Model before rewriting:

find x: set (maxSize 2) of int(1..2)

such that

1 in x

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#packed_1: int(0..3)
new constraints:
  and([sum([(x#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(x#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) >= 0,sum([(x#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(x#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) <= 2;int(1..)])

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x#packed_1

  or([x#packed_1#direct_1,x#packed_1#direct_2,x#packed_1#direct_3,x#packed_1#direct_4;int(1..)])
